**Proof.** `test:house-bot-console` 1.360 (`money.1`): the suite walks every file under `src/app/admin/desk/**` and `src/components/admin/desk*` for a `formatTzs` call, an `.amount` class, a `Tzs`-suffixed prop AND a `title`/`aria-label`/`aria-valuetext` carrying a digit group, FAILS on any site outside the declared role allowlist, and FAILS if the walked population is EMPTY (the section does not exist today, so an absence assertion over it would otherwise pass vacuously). Controls: a planted `formatTzs(book.netTzs)` render and a planted money `title` are both reported.

#### 361. `formatTzs` is the console's only money formatter, and the usage wording is fixed

**Decision.** Every money figure the CONSOLE ITSELF formats goes through `formatTzs` and no other. Refused in every console file: `formatTzsCompact` (except the KPI value slot, 404), `formatTzsSigned`, `formatTzsAbs`, `formatWhole` (it writes a HYPHEN for a negative where money writes U+2212), and any local `toLocaleString` money helper of the `fmt` shape. A pre-built server sentence from `eligibility.ts`, `pause-reasons.ts`, `alert-copy.ts` or `enter-now-decision.ts` is rendered as the string it is and is NOT re-formatted. The usage wording is exactly, English sentence case: `used TZS 42,000 of TZS 50,000` — lower-case "used"; no percentage in the words (the bar carries the proportion, and a rounded percentage prints "100%" at 99.6%, which is not true); no "remaining"; no "left"; no arrow. A count limit uses the same grammar through `adminCount`: `used 12 of 200 bets`.

**Files.** `src/lib/utils.ts:62` (`export function formatTzs(value: number): string {`), `:63` (the U+2212 sign), `:129`, `:208`, `:213`, `:251` · `src/lib/house-bot/rules.ts:238`, `:240` (`const sign = n < 0 ? "-" : "";`) · `src/lib/server/house-bot/eligibility.ts:416` (the local `fmt` this bans for new code).

**Proof.** `test:house-bot-console` 1.361: no console file imports or calls the four banned formatters or declares a local money `toLocaleString` helper, and every caption the console formats matches `/^used (TZS [\d,]+|\d[\d,]*) of (TZS [\d,]+|\d[\d,]*)( — (at|over) the limit)?$/`. Controls: a planted `formatTzsCompact(used)` outside a KPI value, a caption reading "TZS 42,000 / TZS 50,000", and a planted local formatter are each reported.

#### 362. A usage figure is a kit `ProgressBar` with a `caption`; a KPI tile carries at most ONE amount, and none in its `delta`

**Decision.** (a) `src/components/ui/progress-bar.tsx` gains ONE optional prop, `caption?: React.ReactNode`, which REPLACES the component's built-in numeric line when given, rendered as `<p className="text-body-sm text-text-muted">` with the CAP'S NAME first and each amount in its own `<span className="amount">`, the connective words left as normal text so the paragraph may WRAP while each amount stays indivisible. ⛔ **TWO PROPS, NOT ONE — corrected by the completeness review, because the draft's single prop was unbuildable.** `aria-valuetext` is a STRING attribute; a `caption` that must carry `.amount` spans is a `React.ReactNode`, and `aria-valuetext={caption}` would stamp `[object Object]` into the DOM for every screen-reader user and into the probe's scanned body. So the kit extension is exactly TWO optional props added together: `caption?: React.ReactNode` (the painted line, replacing the built-in one) and `captionText?: string` (the same sentence in plain text, used for `aria-valuetext`, and REQUIRED whenever `caption` is given — a caption without it is a TypeScript error via a discriminated pair, or, if that shape is rejected in review, a runtime `throw` in development plus the 420 assertion). The two are built from ONE server expression so they can never disagree: the reader returns the finished plain sentence, and the component wraps its two figures in `.amount` spans for the painted form. `value` and `max` stay raw integers so the aria range still reports real usage; `tone` stays `"brand"`; `label` keeps its `aria-label` job (`progress-bar.tsx:55` ✔). The one existing caller passes no caption and is byte-identical (§K5: extend the kit, never fork it). Because NO suite reads this kit file today, the same commit adds the pin that the built-in line renders only when `caption` is absent. (b) ⛔ No KPI tile carries two amounts, and NO amount appears in a tile's `delta`: the value slot is one `.amount … truncate` element and the delta slot is `font-mono text-micro` (0.4 px tracking on this rung), so a second amount would either clip money (§A5/M4a) or letter-space it (§M4). A tile's single compact amount in the `value` slot is legal and is what 404 renders; the exact `used X of Y` pair lives on the limits and rules tabs, which is DA §K 7d's own division ("a tab may hide a DETAIL, never a STATE").

**Why.** The plan asks for a ProgressBar PLUS a separate `.amount` caption, which would render TWO captions — and the kit's own is `font-mono text-micro uppercase tracking-widest` over `value.toLocaleString()`, i.e. a bare, letter-spaced number with no currency unit, so "120,000 of 500,000" would sit under a bar labelled only to a screen reader (`label` is `aria-label` only). §M4 was made mechanical precisely because a rung's tracking over an amount passed every guard while reading "like a reference code". ⛔ And the whole sentence is NOT wrapped in `.amount`: that class is `white-space: nowrap`, so a 37-character nowrap sentence overflows a 360 card (§A6) — the figures alone are nowrap, the sentence wraps.

**Files.** `src/components/ui/progress-bar.tsx:28`, `:39`, `:45-46`, `:52-54`, `:55` (`label` → `aria-label`), `:66-68` · `src/app/admin/retention/purge-chain-card.tsx:202` (the unchanged caller) · `src/app/globals.css:945` — `.amount.amount { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; letter-spacing: 0; white-space: nowrap; }` · `src/components/admin/admin-shell.tsx:498`, `:543`, `:553` ✔ · `docs/DESIGN_AUTHORITY.md:1934`, `:2261`, `:2300` · `docs/design-system/v2-2026-07-27/02-components/progress-bars/spec.md:12` (the July signature that already carried `label?`/`showValue?`).

**Proof.** `test:house-bot-console` 1.362: every console usage row is a `<ProgressBar>` with a `caption` whose first text node is the cap's name and whose amounts each sit in their own `.amount` span; no `<AdminKpi` in the console receives a `formatTzs`/`Tzs` value in `delta`; no tile receives two amounts; `progress-bar.tsx` renders its built-in line only when `caption` is absent; and the walked population is non-empty. Controls: a caption-plus-built-in double line, an `AdminKpi delta={formatTzs(cap)}`, and an `.amount`-less caption are each reported. Plus `test:type-scale` and `test:contrast` green and the 360/1280 render read for a wrapped, unclipped caption. The kit change lands with its record in the same commit: the progress-bars spec and `07-provenance/CHANGELOG.md`.

#### 363. The per-bot usage card: five money rows, two loss rows, exposure labelled "open now"

**Decision.** `/admin/desk/[id]?tab=overview` carries ONE money card, titled **"Limit usage"**, with exactly these money rows in the seam's own order, each a ProgressBar with a 361 caption: 1. `Daily stake cap` — used = the day book's `stakedTzs`, limit `capDailyStakeTzs`, scope "today"; 2. `Daily loss cap — projected` — `projectedLossTzs` against `capDailyLossTzs`, scope "today", with `LOSS_CAP_HINT`'s own words; 3. `Daily loss cap — settled` — `realisedLossTzs`, same limit, scope "today"; 4. `Open exposure cap` — live open stake against `capOpenExposureTzs`, scope **"open now"**, NOT "today"; 5. `Staff-chosen daily cap` — the staff-chosen placed-today stake against `capStaffChosenDailyTzs`, scope "today", aggregated for the bot and NEVER split by officer (365). The count rows (bets this hour, bets today) use the same component with `adminCount` grammar, from 351's reader. Every used-value is computed INSIDE the gate module and reaches the page as 372's painted row (340). These are the five money caps this card measures; the seam also refuses on `capPerMarketTzs` and `balanceFloorTzs`, which carry no usage row (365, 368). ⛔ STRUCK and never built: 03's `AdminCard "Book"` and PLAN §8's `houseBotBook` line.

**Why.** These are the five money caps whose usage the seam actually refuses on, in the seam's order, read from the one function the gates read — which is `book.ts`'s stated reason for existing. The scope word is not decoration: the open-exposure reader has NO day filter ("Open stake right now, whatever day it was placed"), so an exposure row under a "Today's" heading is a mislabelled figure, a §C2 honesty defect the plan's own card title would have shipped.

#### 364. The limits tab's usage set, and THREE unset-limit captions chosen by membership — never a bar at zero

**Decision.** `/admin/desk?tab=limits` carries one "Limit usage" card whose money rows are the all-bots twins of 363's: `gCapDailyStakeTzs`, `gCapDailyLossTzs` (projected and settled, two rows, one limit), `gCapOpenExposureTzs` (scope "open now"), `gCapStaffChosenDailyTzs`. No usage row for `gCapPerMarketTzs`, `gCounterPerPlayerTzsPerDay` or `gStaffChosenMaxCounterpartyShare` (365). **An unset limit renders NO bar** — a bar at 0% says "headroom" where `over(null, x)` refuses everything — and instead ONE of exactly three captions, chosen by what the code actually does, on the usage row where one exists and otherwise on the FORM FIELD: · a member of `REQUIRED_FOR_MASTER_ON` (which includes `gCapPerMarketTzs` and `gCounterPerPlayerTzsPerDay`) → `Not set — the master switch cannot be turned on.` with the Link to `?tab=limits#limits-first-unset`; · a member of `CLEAR_EXEMPT` (`capStaffChosenPerDay`, `capStaffChosenDailyTzs`, `gCapStaffChosenPerDay`, `gCapStaffChosenDailyTzs`, and the targets fields) → `Not set — targeted and manual stakes cannot be placed.`; · any OTHER per-bot cap the seam reads UNCONDITIONALLY (`stakeMinTzs`, `stakeMaxTzs`, `capPerMarketTzs`, `balanceFloorTzs`, `capDailyStakeTzs`, `capDailyLossTzs`, `capOpenExposureTzs`) → `Not set — this bot cannot place a bet.` ⛔ The draft's blanket "this bot cannot place a bet" for every unset cap is REFUSED: the staff-chosen caps are read only inside `if (f.staffChosen)`, and `CLEAR_EXEMPT`'s own docblock says clearing one "turns Enter now or targets off, which only ever reduces risk" — the blanket caption would be a LIE on the surface whose whole purpose is to stop lies about money. No caption uses the words "house", "bot", "liquidity" or "staff-chosen", each a needle ✔, because this text is a candidate for a client chunk.

#### 365. No per-market, per-player or per-officer money figure anywhere in the console

**Why.** D20b strikes "the house lines on admin screens (R2)", and the REPLAN's Commit 7 default says no per-market house line. A per-market usage figure IS a per-market house line wherever it is painted, and a per-player counter figure is a statement about one named player's exposure to 50pick's own stakes — the crowd-out report Ali dropped. A per-officer split is the struck R1 scorecard by another name.

#### 366. Two loss rows against one cap, and the DISPLAY is clamped at zero — the reader is not

**Decision.** Projected loss and settled loss are TWO usage rows against the ONE `capDailyLossTzs`/`gCapDailyLossTzs`, never collapsed, because the gate and the stop read different figures: the seam refuses a new stake on PROJECTED loss and a bot is auto-paused only on SETTLED loss. At EVERY render site — caption, table cell, attribute — both used-values are clamped to `Math.max(0, …)`, so a cohort in profit reads `used TZS 0 of TZS 50,000` and never a negative or signed amount. The READERS are not clamped: "Realised loss may be negative: that is a profit, and it is reported as such, never clamped" stays true, `foldDayBook` is untouched, and every gate, stop and `test:house-bot-caps` case keeps the signed value.

**Why.** An unclamped loss bar re-creates exactly the figure 266 struck: a negative "used" amount is the day's profit, rendered as "−TZS 12,000 of TZS 50,000" — "Today's net" wearing a cap's label. `ProgressBar` already floors the geometry, so without the clamp the bar and its own caption would disagree, which is worse.

Control: the unclamped render, in a caption AND in a roster cell, is reported in both places.

#### 367. Usage at or over its limit says so in words, at every render site; the bar's tone never changes

**Decision.** When a used amount equals its limit the usage text appends ` — at the limit`; when it exceeds it, ` — over the limit`. The text always states the TRUE used amount, because the bar saturates at 100% and cannot distinguish 100% from 140%. This binds EVERY usage site: the ProgressBar caption, an `.admin-tbl` usage cell (373, where there is no bar at all and the clause is the only signal), and `captionText` — the plain string the bar's `aria-valuetext` takes (362, corrected: a ReactNode caption cannot be an aria attribute). The tone stays `"brand"` in every state — claret is reserved for irreversible ceremony and the AUTO_PAUSED chip, the component has no warning tone, and colour is never this signal (§A4).

**Why.** Usage can legitimately exceed a limit (a cap lowered mid-day, or stakes placed while it was higher) and `over()` then refuses everything, which is a state an officer must be able to read. A saturated bar with a truthful caption is honest; a saturated bar alone is a misreport of the §M4a family.

**Proof.** `test:house-bot-console` 1.367: for used = limit and used = limit + 1, every render site's text ends in the exact clause and carries the true amount in full; no console `<ProgressBar>` passes `tone="claret"`. #### 368. No wallet balance on a running bot's page — the floor STATE instead; the wizard keeps the one plain balance

**Decision.** `/admin/desk/[id]` shows NO balance amount. In its place, one server-built sentence: `Balance is above the floor of TZS 5,000.` or `Balance is below the floor of TZS 5,000 — this bot cannot place a bet.`, and when `balanceFloorTzs` is null, 364's unconditional-cap caption. The FLOOR is a configured limit and may be named; the holder's balance is not shown, compacted, or put in a tile. S3's "Live balance" KPI and PLAN:439's strip balance are STRUCK. **The ONE exception is `/admin/desk/new`'s account-check card**, which keeps `Live balance` as a plain `formatTzs` amount in `.amount text-title-sm text-text` (never gold) plus the bonus line captioned "Not usable by the bot": at designate time the bot has no caps at all — every one is nullable and unset — so there is no configured limit to measure the balance against, and the officer is deciding whether this account can fund anything. That figure reaches the wizard ONLY through 359's gate reader. ⛔ **RECORDED AS A DEVIATION FROM RULING 266, not as an application of it (the completeness review's correction).** 266's words are "money ONLY as usage against a configured limit … written as 'used X of Y'", and a bare balance is neither; 266 outranks this file (§0), so this exception is stated as a deviation with its reason on the record and put to Ali as **§7 Q8** rather than decided silently. If he refuses it, the check card carries the balance as a STATE instead — "funded" / "empty" — and 03:197 is struck with it; the wizard remains buildable either way, and nothing else in this file depends on the amount.

**Why.** `BALANCE_FLOOR` is a real gate, so the officer must know which side of it the account is on — and that is a STATE, not an amount, and the state is the whole control. Publishing the amount instead adds a holder's wallet position to a payload that, per 259, is streamed to any signed-in account: a player's finances leaking to other players, which is a bigger defect than any house figure. Role D is unaffected: a refusal may still name money (`eligibility.ts` already does).

**Decision.** (a) The `?tab=money` tab is not built: no holder transaction list, no `formatTzsSigned` amounts, no OWNER_MONEY rows, no "No money movements since designation on {date}" empty state; the tab appears in no rail, no `ROUTE_KEYS` entry, no `scripts/design-gate/routes.mjs` entry and no `test:tab-anchors` subject. Because that route does not exist on disk today, the re-point and the route land in the SAME commit and `test:house-bot-comms`' SETTLE_BLOCKED case is updated in it. (c) The `history` tab prints the lexicon word, actor, from → to and reason only — NO amount from an event payload, specifically not OWNER_MONEY's `amountTzs` or `balanceTzs`; an OWNER_MONEY row carries a `.row-link` to `/admin/transactions?q=<txnId>`, which is where the bell and email already send the officer.

It is also the console's largest 259 exposure: its payload is one player's deposit and withdrawal history streamed to any signed-in account. Ali's own D20 answer on this very tab was to strike its house chip; striking the rest is the same decision applied to the same surface — and it is offered back to him as §7 Q5.

#### 372. Every console money figure is produced by a QUERY-shaped gate reader, and a failed read is never a zero

It decides through `houseConsoleAudience` before it reads anything, returns the rows whole for the audience and `null` otherwise, and 355 governs its failures. (b) The row shape carries only what is painted — `{ label, usedTzs, limitTzs, scope, state }` — no `FieldId`, no cap column name, no bot id, no route key, because anything the console puts in a client chunk or a flight payload is reachable by a signed-in player. (c) A read that FAILS renders `couldn't read — this is not zero` with no bar, and a whole-panel failure renders `AdminLoadError what="limit usage"`; never `used TZS 0 of TZS 50,000` — the platform's own law (`AdminKpi`'s `unavailable` exists so "a failed money/analytics read never shows a fabricated TZS 0 as if it were real" ✔), and `houseDayBook` genuinely throws on a malformed day key as well as on its store read. (d) SAME COMMIT: `houseUsageForConsole: 3` joins `CONSOLE_GATES` ✔, 0.260.1's `consoleGateCalls` expectation for it is set to the measured count with no existing floor lowered, and the module header's "the AUDIT ROWS a console page renders, and nothing else" ✔ is rewritten to name the usage reader — otherwise the gate law does not cover the new door at all.

#### 373. Money second in tables, one grammar, and the feed's header is `Stake` — never "House stake"

**Decision.** (a) In every console table money is the SECOND column (and third where two money columns exist), and each money cell is `<td className="tabular text-right"><span className="amount">…</span></td>` — the shipped `Amt` shape — because the second column is the ANSWER column, measured at 360 on `/admin/house`. **No money-bearing console table carries a `min-w-*`:** `admin-tbl` is `width: 100%`, so a min-width stretches every column and pushes the answer out of a narrow card (measured there: 4 of that card's 6 money cells out of view at 360 WITH the reorder in place); `ScrollX` carries the width and `td.tabular`'s nowrap keeps each amount one object. A wide NON-money console table may keep a min-width only if a 360 render shows no money cell pushed out of view — `/admin/house` itself keeps `min-w-[560px]` and `min-w-[1080px]` on three tables, so the ⛔ is scoped to the money card, not global. (b) The roster keeps exactly TWO money columns, second and third: **`Loss today (projected)`** — the PROJECTED loss used of `capDailyLossTzs` — and `Exposure` (open now, used of `capOpenExposureTzs`), each in 361's ONE grammar with 367's clause and 366's clamp. ⛔ **The basis is PROJECTED and it is NAMED IN THE HEADER — corrected by the completeness review, which found 310 saying `projectedLossTzs` and this ruling saying "settled loss" for the same column.** Projected is right for the roster and the band alike because projected is the basis the seam REFUSES a new stake on (`cap-precheck.ts:55` ✔), and the roster answers "what will stop a bet". The SETTLED figure is not lost and is not hidden: it is 363's second loss row on the bot page, the basis the auto-pause fires on, and 366 keeps the two rows separate there precisely because one cap has two readings. A roster column headed `Loss today` with no basis is the ambiguity 366 exists to prevent, so the word is in the header, not only in the caption. **Because two nowrap amount PAIRS in columns 2 and 3 risk the very defect measured on `/admin/house`, the roster's money cells are READ at 360 before this checkpoint closes; if the third money cell is out of view, the cap moves into the column header (`Loss today / cap 50,000`) and the cell carries the used figure alone.** `Today net` and `Live balance` are struck (360, 368). (c) The activity feed's money column header is `Stake`, never "House stake": `house[ -]?stakes?` is a needle ✔, so that header would fail the branch's own bundle scan the moment it reached a client chunk or a prerendered document, and would be a house word in a payload a signed-in player can pull.

#### 374. The proof instrument: the new suite, its gold scan, its population floor, and a CANARY amount in the served probe

**Decision.** 318 creates `test:house-bot-console`; it carries every `money.*` section above, each with a CONTROL that plants the defect and requires it to be reported, PLUS a POPULATION FLOOR: every section names the files it walked and FAILS if that set is empty, so no absence assertion can pass on a folder that does not exist yet ✔. It also carries 03 §5 trap 8's gold scan — no `gold`, `gilt`, `Chip gold|resolved`, `AdminKpi gold`, `Callout tone="gold"`, `Toggle tone="gold"` or `ReceiptRow emphasis="total"` anywhere in the console's files — because `test:gold-is-money` is scoped to two identity files and could never report it. `qa:house-bot-console-probe`'s fixture is extended so that every money quantity 360 permits is planted as a DISTINCT canary amount that occurs nowhere else on the platform; each candidate is first SHOWN ABSENT from the repo's fixtures and seeds, or the probe's zero is meaningless. The probe then asserts, for the player, the holder and the trigger player, that no canary digit string appears in any body from any `/admin/desk` route, tab, flight or admin API GET — and, as the control, that the ADMIN's body DOES carry them on the pages that show them; a route instance whose ADMIN body carries none prints NOT MEASURED, never a pass.

**Why.** The probe's present needle is the shared vocabulary plus the bot's label and id — a TZS figure is NONE of those, so today's pass proves nothing about a leaked money figure. A unique amount is the only needle that can be looked for, and the ADMIN control is what makes a zero worth anything ("a sweep reporting zero is worth nothing until it has been shown to detect one"; TikTok's HTTP 200).

### Area 5 — D19 for the console itself, rulings 380–399

#### 380. The gate is in the PAGE, on the stored role, with a literal route — and `loading.tsx` is not a gate site

**Decision.** Every console page, route handler and server action begins by awaiting the audience verdict through `house-console-read.ts` with a STRING-LITERAL route (340, 343), BEFORE any house read. A false verdict returns the restricted panel with an EMPTY view model: no bot label, no id, no figure, no sentence, no count, no tab count, no crumb value and no client prop. ⛔ `loading.tsx` is NOT a gate site — a Suspense fallback has no session and renders before any read — so it is governed by 313 instead: the roster and wizard loaders carry the REAL neutral head, and the detail loader carries no title string at all. ⛔ The route argument is a module constant, never `headers().get("x-pathname")`: the gate-call pin requires a literal ✔, and a header-keyed gate would ALSO fail closed for Ali the moment the header were missing, making the probe's zero-hit verdict vacuous. `force-dynamic` on every route is part of this ruling, not only a liveness matter: without it a console route can be statically prerendered at build time, landing house data in the PRERENDERED DOCUMENTS population the bundle scan reads, for a page whose gate never ran.

**Proof.** `qa:house-bot-console-probe` 4.1 with every console instance in `MUST_CARRY` (315, 392) — so an ADMIN-silent console page FAILS and a vacuous zero cannot read as a pass. `test:house-bot-console` 1.380: every page/route/action names a gate before its first house read, no file passes a header value as `route`, every route file declares `force-dynamic`, and the loaders have zero vocabulary hits.

#### 381. ONE neutral codename, and it is `desk`; three server-rendered strings are NOT free

**Decision.** The route segment, nav `key`, `ROUTE_KEYS` prefix and key, any `CRUMB_LABELS` entry, the `NAV_GROUPS` `label`, every client component/file/directory name, every React context name, every client prop name and every browser-storage key prefix carry NO vocabulary word. ONE codename is used for all of them and it is **`desk`** (320) — ⛔ the draft's `exposure` is REFUSED: the console renders "Open exposure" figures on every tab and S6's "resolver exposure display" was struck by name, so a section called Exposure beside a KPI called Open exposure is a naming collision on the one page where a figure must never be ambiguous. **And three strings are not free even though they are server-rendered, each taking the codename:** (a) the route's `export const metadata = { title: … }`, because the document `<title>` is emitted into a response served 200 to any signed-in account (the pattern is `export const metadata = { title: "Admin · House" };`); (b) the `loading.tsx` head (313, 380); (c) the section gate's restricted-panel heading, which is computed from the last URL segment ✔ — a second, independent reason the console adds no `CRUMB_LABELS` entry. What remains free is only what the gate withholds: the page's own H1 and body copy, the card titles, the feed sentences and the chips (§7 Q2). ⛔ The codename is NOT added to `HOUSE_BENIGN_SAMPLES`: that list holds LOOK-ALIKES that must not match (`HOUSE_FEE`, `/admin/house`, `House edge`, `hb_nonce`, `Xhb_…`) ✔, and `desk` resembles no needle, so the entry would be a control that cannot fail.

**Why.** Measured: `admin-nav-groups.ts`'s `ROUTE_KEYS` and `CRUMB_LABELS` ship verbatim in a publicly downloadable chunk, and the module is value-imported by three `"use client"` files; `roles.ts` likewise through `roles-matrix.tsx`. NAV_GROUPS' labels are tree-shaken out today (three label markers measured absent from the built chunks) — but tree-shaking is not a contract and `test:house-bot-disclosure` 1.1 walks the SOURCE, so the label stays neutral rather than relying on the shake.

**Proof.** `verify:house-bot-bundle` 0 hits across `.next/static` (paths included), the prerendered documents and `public/`, with its planted-path control intact; `test:house-bot-disclosure` 1.1; `test:house-bot-console` 1.381 — the console's `metadata.title`, its loaders and the crumb label for its prefix are all vocabulary-free, and `desk` is a hit in no family.

#### 382. Every console action export name and every guard label is house-free

**Decision.** No server action the console adds may have an export name containing a vocabulary word (so `getHouseBotStatusAction`, `startHouseBotAction` and every sibling are forbidden; the neutral form is named from the codename). Separately, the `action` string each console action passes to `requireOwner`/`requireStaff`/`requireHouseOwner` must ALSO be house-free, because a refused non-owner caller's SECURITY row records it in `targetId` and `payload.action`, and the player's own GDPR export reads exactly those rows. `requireHouseOwner`'s own identifier may keep its name: it lives in `src/lib/server/rbac-guard.ts`, which no client may import, and `requireOwner` is measured ABSENT from the public chunks.

**Why.** Measured: server-action export names survive verbatim into publicly downloadable chunks (`setRoleGrant`, `resetRoleGrants`, `addStaffByPhone`, `setStaffRole`, `buildDsarBundleAction` each found in `.next/static`). And measured in the code path: `requireOwner`/`requireStaff` write `privilege_escalation_blocked` with `targetId: action`; `privilege_escalation_blocked` is in no `HOUSE_AUDIT` key set and so is NOT excluded from `OWN_AUDIT_EXCLUDED_ACTIONS`; `withoutHouseAuditKeys` rewrites payload keys and `reason` only, never `action` or `targetId`; and `exportUserData` is PLAYER-triggered from `/profile/account`. Adding `privilege_escalation_blocked` to the exclusion list is NOT an option — that hides a security row from its own subject. **Measured boundary, recorded:** the leak is confined to the DOWNLOADABLE export, not the screen — the second reader `getOwnActivity` feeds `/profile/account`, which renders `auditActionLabel(...)` and falls back to the CATEGORY word for an unknown action, so a house-named label would print as "Security" there and appear only in the JSON.

#### 383. Every console action is a player-callable endpoint, and its refusal is not an oracle

**Decision.** Every console action independently (a) resolves the caller's STORED role through `requireHouseOwner`/`requireOwner` — never the session cookie's role field — BEFORE any read, password check, lock or write; (b) returns, for a caller outside the audience, ONE fixed typed refusal `{ok:false, code:"NOT_OWNER"}` whose message is the platform's existing neutral sentence, carrying no vocabulary word, no `field`, no `href`, no bot id, no count and no existence signal — the SAME answer for "you are not the owner", "no such bot" and "that bot was removed"; (c) writes nothing and locks nothing on that path (the SECURITY row of 382 is the only side effect); (d) is never passed the bot id, route or audience as a TRUSTED argument — the id is validated against a read done after the gate; (e) returns no partial payload to a non-owner on any code path, including a thrown-then-caught branch. The same no-oracle rule binds the PAGES (399). `NOT_OWNER` carries no `href`; `REAUTH_*` for a real owner may keep theirs.

**Why.** Measured: 200 of the 221 server-action ids in this build's `server-reference-manifest.json` appear in the publicly downloadable `.next/static` chunks, so any visitor can read every action id off the bundle and any signed-in account can POST it. The action's own guard is the ONLY protection — there is no layout, no nav and no page in that path.

**Files.** `src/lib/server/rbac-guard.ts:208`, `:210` · `.next/server/server-reference-manifest.json` (221 ids under `node`, each with `filename`, `exportedName` and a `workers` route map) · `plans/house-bots/04-amendments.md:1045` (`requireHouseOwner()` — the one owner predicate; never redirects or throws).

**Proof.** `qa:house-bot-console-probe` 6.act (394): every console action id POSTed as a player, the holder, a trigger player and a non-owner staff account — each answered with a refusal, no vocabulary hit in the body, and ZERO change in the HouseBot / intent / event / control / audit row counts taken either side of the pass, with an ADMIN control proving one read action does return house data. `test:house-bot-console` 1.383 pins the guard as the first statement and the single shared refusal shape.

#### 384. No console client module reaches a house module — not by value import, not by type import, not by copy

**Decision.** No `"use client"` file the console adds, and nothing in its import graph, may import ANY module under `src/lib/house-bot/`, `src/lib/server/house-bot/`, `src/lib/server/house-bot-dal.ts` or `src/lib/server/house-console-read.ts` — by value import OR by `import type`. Every client component receives a plain view model of primitives and formatted strings from ONE neutral module whose type and field names carry no vocabulary word. `feed-copy.ts`, `alert-copy.ts`, `pause-reasons.ts`, `rules.ts`, `constants.ts` and the status lexicon stay SERVER-IMPORT-ONLY: the rules form renders from a server-serialised neutral field descriptor list, never from `FIELD_META`; the feed table is a server component and the client owns only the countdown and the Cancel trigger; the chip's word and variant come from `HOUSE_BOT_STATUS_DISPLAY` on the server (311). ⛔ The draft's claimed licence is WITHDRAWN: `PLAN.md:695` reads "Client/server boundary: pure modules carry no `\"use client\"` and no server imports, and a build is not a render" — the phrase "pure, so either side may import them" appears in NO plan document, so there is nothing to deviate from and this ruling merely pins what the code already forbids. **When 1.2's module-path regex is widened, the allowlist literal in `scripts/lib/house-bot-reports-cases.mts:453` is rewritten in the SAME commit**, because `0.175.allow` asserts every allowlisted pattern is still declared, in its own file, and that the lists may only SHRINK.

**Why.** 1.2 already forbids any `house-bot/` module being client-reachable, and measured, every one of them is saturated: `rules.ts` ~191 hits, `constants.ts` ~192, `alert-copy.ts` 89, `feed-copy.ts` 41, `pause-reasons.ts` 28 (only `consent.ts` 0). Type-only imports are erased by the bundler and invisible to the walker (its own 2.c3/2.c6 controls), which makes them the exact shape that survives review and becomes a value import in one careless edit — so the pin is on the import SPECIFIER, which catches both.

#### 385. Copy provenance: no sentence the console's server copy can emit appears in a client module or the public bundle

**Decision.** A new proof in both the fast suite and the bundle scan: extract every one-line string literal of 25 characters or more from the console's server copy modules (`feed-copy.ts`, `alert-copy.ts`, `pause-reasons.ts`, the status lexicon and the console's own copy module) BY PARSING the module — never a line-crossing regex — and assert that none appears (a) in any module reachable from a `"use client"` entry, or (b) anywhere in `.next/static`, the prerendered documents or `public/`. #### 386. The live-refresh trigger names no notification kind

**Decision.** The console's client strip mounts `useEventStream()` and listens for the existing generic window event — **`50pick:sse:notification`**, the name `EVENT_MAP` gives `notification:new` (`use-event-stream.ts:22-27` ✔); ⛔ NOT the literal `notification:new`, which is the wire type and is dispatched on `window` by nothing — then refreshes WITHOUT inspecting `detail.notification.kind`. The filter buys nothing: `notification:new` is in `USER_SCOPED`, so the SSE route forwards a notification only to the account it belongs to, and every house alert goes to admins only (D19c). And `notification-filters.ts` is ONE value import from the client graph — it imports `MONEY_KINDS` from `comms-registry` and is out of the population today only because its importers happen to carry no `"use client"` directive.

#### 388. Dialog and modal copy comes from the server as props, and no typed word names a bot

**Decision.** Every console `Modal`, `ConfirmDialog`, Callout, empty state, disabled reason and overlay sentence is built on the SERVER and handed to the client as `title`, `body`, `confirmLabel`, `children` or a named string prop; no console client file contains a sentence about bots, a status word, a cap name, a pause reason or an engine-health line. Where a client component must choose between sentences for a state it computes itself (pending, "Firing", "Already firing", the 8 s and 45 s uncertain-result copy, the restored-draft line), it receives the ALTERNATIVES as props from the same server render. The master switch's typed confirmation word is NOT "BOTS ON": it is a neutral word supplied as a prop (`SWITCH ON`), and `REMOVE` stays. `HouseBotFormContext` is renamed to the codename's neutral form. Prop NAMES are bound by 381.

**Why.** Measured: client component prop NAMES survive minification into public chunks (`mayAct`, `domainLabel`, `subjectId`, `serverNow`, `alreadyClosed` each found in `.next/static`; `viewDomains` absent), so a prop named for the feature is published even when its value is not. A sentence typed into a client file is published outright, and 385 measured that most such sentences would not be caught by the vocabulary. "BOTS ON" is not a needle ✔ — which is precisely why it must not be relied on: it would ship a bot-shaped confirmation phrase to every visitor with no guard able to see it. The pattern is the neutral-slot one C5 step 5 already established.

Recorded for the next reader: the freshness half already EXISTS (the script prints NOT MEASURED when the build is older than a file under `src/`), so that clause restates the code rather than adding to it — and this worktree's build is older than files C5-5b wrote today, which means every bundle figure quoted anywhere in this review came from a build the script itself would REFUSE. That is exactly why the presence control is necessary. Also recorded: in this configuration `.next/static` holds flat hashed filenames with no route-derived directory, so the planted path control is latent rather than live — and it STAYS, because a Next or config change can restore named chunks.

Mutation: run it against a build made before the console existed — NOT MEASURED, not a pass.

**Decision.** (a) `scripts/house-bot-console-probe.mts` joins `VOCABULARY_CONSUMERS`: it already imports `houseHits` and declares no pattern of its own, and it is an absence consumer the single-source pin does not cover today. #### 399. A missing record and a refused viewer answer identically: the console's pages are not an id oracle

**Decision.** For a viewer OUTSIDE the audience, every console page answers identically for "no such bot", "that bot was removed" and "not the audience" — the same status, the same body, the same length. `notFound()` for an unknown id (302) runs only AFTER the audience verdict, so the 404/200 difference is visible to the ADMIN alone. ⛔ Without this, a signed-in player reaching the page through the 259 hole could enumerate every live bot id by status code — with no house word anywhere in either body, and therefore invisible to the probe's needle test, which scans for words, labels and ids and not for a status pattern.

#### 410. Filters are `FilterPill rank="dense"` + `DateTimeRangeFilter rank="dense"` in ONE `data-filter-rail`, URL-backed with `replace`, and the file is REGISTERED

**Decision.** The activity feed's filters are dense `FilterPill` groups for Bot, Product and Outcome, each introduced by `FilterGroupKey`, plus `DateTimeRangeFilter rank="dense" presetIds={["today","24h","7d"]}`, all inside one `<div data-filter-rail className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">`. `QueryStrip` is NOT used (no caller under `src/app/admin`). The 32 px density comes from `rank="dense"` and nothing else — no `h-[32px]`, no inline style, no per-surface variant. **The filter file is added to `ADMIN_SURFACES` in `scripts/filter-language.test.mts` in the same commit**, or the new rail is invisible to the gate that owns it. Only the SELECTED pill carries an outline.

#### 411. Pagination is the kit pager at 20, carrying every live filter, and its ghost is drawn only where the list is unbounded

**Decision.** The activity feed and the history tab page with `AdminPagination page total perPage={PER_PAGE} baseHref={buildBaseHref("/admin/desk", {tab, …filters})}`, using `parsePage`; `PER_PAGE` is taken from the primitive (20), never re-typed; every filter in force is carried in `baseHref` so paging never silently clears one, and changing a filter resets `page`. The loader draws a pager ghost ONLY where the row source is unbounded (the feed and the history). The roster gets none — not because it holds 5 rows, but because `maxDesignatedBots`' CEILING is 20, and 20 rows at 20 per page is a single page, so the pager returns null and a forced ghost would over-draw 77 px.

#### 412. Forms: one `FormColumn measure="form"`, every control `size="md"`, one guarded form per tab, and a refusal that lands on the right field on the right tab

**Decision.** Every form sits in `FormColumn measure="form"` (640) and lays numeric caps out as `grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3`. Every control is `size="md"` (44 px) with `Button size="md"` beside it. ⛔ `size="sm"` is used NOWHERE in the console, because the `sm` rungs disagree across atoms (Input 40, the others 36) — and the shipped `<Input mono size="sm" …>` at `src/app/admin/agents/agents-client.tsx:59` is cited as an INSTANCE OF THAT DEFECT, not as a precedent. Money inputs are `Input size="md" mono prefix="TZS" inputMode="numeric"` inside `Field label hint error dataField`. Exactly ONE guarded form per tab (`PendingChangesBar` is a singleton) wrapped in `UnsavedChangesGuard dirty`; the holder-password field is never `name`/`autoComplete` `password`. A refused save returns `{field}`, and the client calls `focusFirstInvalid` and HANDLES ITS RESULT UNION: on `{ ok: false, reason: "not-rendered", ownedByTab }` it navigates to `ownedByTab` and re-calls after the render lands — the tab comes from the helper's own answer, never re-derived at the call site (the helper's `else`-less form is the HISTORICAL defect, already fixed and named in its own header as defect ③). **The UserPicker is part of this ruling:** an in-flow `<ul role="listbox">` with no portal and no scroll box of its own, at most 10 options, each `li role="option"` at least 44 px tall, ineligible options `aria-disabled` with the reason wrapped beneath, a polite live count, and the holder's phone rendered through the SERVER `Sensitive` component (359 — never `SensitiveReveal` at the call site, which would skip `readCell`). Every modal or reason-Textarea file exempt from the unsaved-changes population is listed in EXEMPT ① WITH its reason, and no floor is lowered.

#### 413. Status and result words and their chip variants come from ONE server-side map; PENDING is NEUTRAL

**Decision.** A bot's status renders `<Chip size="sm" variant={HOUSE_BOT_STATUS_DISPLAY[status].chip}>{…word}</Chip>` — ACTIVE green→`success`, PAUSED amber→`warning`, AUTO_PAUSED claret→`claret`, REMOVED slate→`neutral`. The feed's result word and variant come from a SIBLING server map in the same module: placed→`pending` (royal), **pending→`neutral` (slate)**, skipped→`neutral`, failed→`danger`, cancelled→`neutral`; CLAIMED renders the plain word "Firing". ⛔ The draft's royal PENDING is REFUSED: PLAN §8 reads "placed royal, skipped/pending neutral, failed danger, never gold", and royal there creates a placed/pending collision that then needs an §A4 workaround the plan's own assignment does not create. `gold`, `resolved`, `objection`, `yes` and `no` are used nowhere in the console. No variant is typed beside a label. `src/lib/status-tone.ts` gains NO `HOUSE_BOT*` key and no exception entry naming a bot, and `src/lib/admin-status-lexicon.ts` gains no house word — both are value-imported by client components. `status-display.ts` survives C5-5b on TWO measured remaining callers; if it is removed anyway, Commit 7 re-creates it in the same place with the same shape (311).

#### 414. Engine, auto-off and change notices are kit Callouts on a fixed tone table; only the danger rows announce

**Decision.** The engine notice is `Callout tone size="md" surface="panel" emphasis="strong" role="alert" title meta`, above the KPI band on both pages, with the tone and copy taken ROW FOR ROW from 04 C11's engine-health table and 03 S5: poller stale or a poller error newer than its beat → `danger`; planner stale only → `warning`; engine disabled by configuration, under the boot grace, or ON with 0 active bots → `neutral`; several instances → a caption line; a beat that could NOT be read → `danger` with `meta="Last seen: unknown"` (§C2: an unreadable health state never renders as healthy). `role="alert"` is passed ONLY on the danger rows — a `warning` or `neutral` row takes the default `note`, because an alert that fires on every page load trains an officer to ignore the one that matters — and the Callout is stably keyed so a refresh does not re-announce it. The auto-off Callout is 308's. Times are formatted on the server. No Callout uses `tone="gold"`, and no dialog body contains `truncate` or `line-clamp-*`. ⛔ The poller rule is 04 C11's, not 03 S4's: the status action is polled every 20 s whether or not a form is dirty, the strip shows "Updated HH:MM:SS EAT", and a change arriving while dirty shows a warning Callout instead of refreshing under the operator. ⛔ The STALENESS VERDICT itself is 353's (planner beat only, with boot grace) — C11's table says WHICH tone, 353 says WHEN.

#### 416. Every empty state names its cause; a failed read is never painted as empty or as zero; and the not-found surface is named

**Decision.** Each zero-row surface renders its CAUSE with 310's precedence: switch off → "House bots are off — no bot will place a bet"; no bots → `AdminTableEmpty` with the primary "Designate an account"; filtered to nothing → "No rows match these filters" plus "Clear filters" ONLY when clearing actually yields rows; no bets yet → "No house bets yet — set rules, then Start"; history → "No changes yet". A FAILED read renders `AdminLoadError what="…"`, never an empty state; a failed KPI read passes `unavailable` ✔, never a fabricated TZS 0; a failed tab count renders its own `AdminLoadError`; an unknown single value renders an em-dash plus a labelled state (§C2: never a guessed, placeholder or zero-as-unknown number). An unknown or malformed id is `notFound()` AFTER the audience verdict (399), and **the section's not-found surface is the app-root one — no `not-found.tsx` is added here (325) — so the id-shape check runs BEFORE any read and the 404 page carries no id and no house word.** A REMOVED bot renders read-only with a `neutral` Callout and no action buttons (358).

Because `test:measure` excludes admin routes from the TIER-parity comparison BY DESIGN ✔, the loader/page parity is asserted by `test:house-bot-console` at source level AND read as a render at 360 and 1280 (DG-P-04).

#### 419. The capture protocol: six mandatory widths, the locale exception, and what is NOT MEASURED

**Decision.** The build is accepted only with captures READ (opened and looked at) on the house fixture, at ALL SIX widths the plan already mandates — **360, 640, 768, 1024, 1280, 1920** (PLAN §8's own list and 03's six responsive rows; the DoD's four plus the `sm` 640 and `lg` 1024 step boundaries, where the KPI band goes 2-up → 4-up and the `sm:grid-cols-2` form and `<dl>` go 1-up → 2-up) — for every state: roster × {switch OFF, ON, auto-off, engine-stale} × {roster, activity, limits, history}; wizard × {find, check with blocking rows, consent, review}; detail × {PAUSED(NEW), ACTIVE, AUTO_PAUSED(PASSWORD_CHANGED), REMOVED} × {overview, rules, targets, activity, history}; plus the 0-row and failed-read variant of every panel. **LOCALES: English for every state, ⛔ EXCEPT the activity tab's filter rail, which is ALSO captured at 360 in SW and ZH** — measured, `DateTimeRangeFilter` calls `useT()` and 410 renders it at `rank="dense"` (32 px), so its translated preset labels are the one console string that can break §A5's +40% and §A6's zero-overflow. The rest of the console is locale-invariant: measured, only four files under `src/app/admin/` reference `useT`/`getServerT`, and `AdminPageHead`'s `sw` is a hardcoded literal. That narrowing is the DoD's own "deliberate exception, re-baselined with a written reason", and the English-only convention is cited to `scripts/failure-reasons.test.mts:1201-1202` and `:1296` — RECORDED, NOT ASSERTED — with the deviation from `DESIGN_AUTHORITY.md:2001`'s stale citation recorded. 320 px is captured as an overflow CONTROL only, and its known 21 px shell-header overflow is recorded as inherited, never this commit's defect. The static harness runs against THIS build's `next start` sheet with `--sheet-missing` required to turn every assertion red; Modal, Select listbox, overlay, `PendingChangesBar` and the picker listbox print NOT MEASURED there and are verified in the real-route client pass at 360/768/1280; `qa:chaos` and `qa:pending-bar` run once. A green gate is never the evidence: every PNG named above is opened and read, and any width, locale or state not captured is written into `plans/house-bots/DEFERRED-TESTS.md` with its command and why the skip is safe (ruling 275).

### Area 7 — what the completeness review found UNDECIDED, rulings 420–431 (423–430 left as room)

#### 420. A staff ACTOR is named the way `/admin/audit` already names one — by id, never by a name, phone or email

**Decision.** Every place the console names the person who did something — 306's ON sentence ("switched by …"), the history tab's **By** column (317), a cancelled intent's actor, an event's actor — renders the actor's **id**, exactly as the platform's own audit table does, and renders `System — house bot engine` when the actor is null. ⛔ No `db.user.findById` for an actor, no `displayName`, no `displayLabel`, no phone, no email, and no second read of any kind: the id is already on the row. ⛔ And NOT `playerHandle` for a staff actor: `playerHandle` derives "Player #TAIL" from a userId and would print a STAFF officer as a player, which is a false statement about a person on the one screen that records who moved money.

**Why.** The draft left this undecided in three places while 346's pin forbids the read that would have answered it: the control row stores only `switchedById` (`prisma/schema.prisma:1945` ✔) and an event stores only an actor id, so without a decision an implementer must either invent a lookup (a PII read on a route 259 streams) or print nothing. The precedent is measured and needs no new code: `/admin/audit` renders `actor: (e) => e.actorId ?? ""` ✔ — the platform already shows an officer as an id in its own record of who did what, and the console is the same kind of record. A name would also outlive the account (346's own reason for `playerHandle` on the holder side).

**Files.** `src/app/admin/audit/page.tsx:101` ✔ — `    actor: (e) => e.actorId ?? "",` (the precedent) · `prisma/schema.prisma:1944-1947` ✔ — `  switchedAt`, `  switchedById`, `  switchedReason`, and the `offCause` comment · `src/lib/display-label.ts:44` ✔ — `  return \`Player #${tail}\`;` (what this ruling refuses for a staff actor) · `src/lib/server/house-bot-dal.ts:1410` — `HouseBotEventStore.append`'s `actorId`.

**Proof.** `test:house-bot-console` 1.420: the strip's ON sentence and every history row's **By** cell render the stored actor id verbatim for a non-null actor and `System — house bot engine` for null; a spy records ZERO `db.user.findById` attributable to actor naming across a render of the strip plus 20 history rows; and a source pin that no file under the section or in the console's gated readers names `displayLabel`, `displayName`, `phoneE164`, `email` or `playerHandle` for an ACTOR (346 already pins the holder side). Control: a planted name lookup for the switcher is reported.

#### 421. A house schema that is not ready is a STATE, not a crash and not a zero

**Decision.** `houseBotControlStore.get()` THROWS `HouseSchemaNotReady` when the `global` row is missing (`house-bot-dal.ts:1293` ✔, thrown at `:3101` ✔) — which is the state of any database the house migration has not reached, the scratch database included. So `houseRosterForConsole` catches exactly that class, and the page renders: `AdminPageHead` as normal, ONE `Callout tone="neutral"` stating that the feature's tables are not present on this database and that nothing can bet, NO KPI band (not four `unavailable` tiles — nothing failed), NO strip Toggle (there is no control row to switch), and `AdminTableEmpty` naming that cause for the roster. ⛔ It is never rendered as an error boundary, never as `AdminLoadError`, never as TZS 0, and never as an empty roster with no cause. The verdict comes from the thrown class or from `houseBotSchemaReady()` (`src/lib/server/house-bot/schema-ready.ts:92` ✔, returning `HouseSchemaState` at `:44` ✔) — ONE of the two, decided in the gate module, never both, and never inferred from an empty list.

**Why.** Step 1 is built and first rendered against a database, and the control read is its FIRST read; an unhandled `HouseSchemaNotReady` renders `src/app/admin/error.tsx` for the Owner and teaches the session that the page is broken when the database is merely un-migrated. It is also the one failure whose honest answer is neither "failed" nor "zero" — Ali's "'Not applicable' is the most dangerous silent verdict", in the one shape where the verdict is genuinely "not applicable yet". 355 governs every OTHER failure; this ruling exists because a schema state is not a failed read.

**Files.** `src/lib/server/house-bot-dal.ts:942` ✔ — `export class HouseSchemaNotReady extends Error {`; `:1293` ✔ — `  /** Fresh read of the \`global\` row. A missing row throws \`HouseSchemaNotReady\`. */`; `:3101` ✔ — `    if (!rows[0]) throw new HouseSchemaNotReady(\`HouseBotControl '${HOUSE_CONTROL_ID}'\`);` · `src/lib/server/house-bot/schema-ready.ts:44`, `:92` ✔ · `src/lib/server/house-bot/engine-health.ts:31` ✔ — `      return { readable: true, schema: await houseBotSchemaReady(), engine: houseBotEngineHealth() };` (the same state, already read for the S5 Callout).

**Proof.** `test:house-bot-console` 1.421: with the control read rejecting `HouseSchemaNotReady`, the ADMIN's page renders the neutral schema Callout, no `role="progressbar"`, no KPI tile at all, no Toggle and no "TZS" anywhere; with a generic rejection it renders 355's failure treatment instead (the two are not collapsed); and a non-audience viewer's body is unchanged in both (399). Controls: an unhandled throw (the page reaching `error.tsx`) and four `unavailable` tiles are each reported.

#### 422. The seven states are decided, and two of them are decided BY CONSTRUCTION — stated so nobody re-decides them

**Decision.** Every state a console surface can be in has exactly one home, and this ruling is the roll-call the build checks itself against: **loading** → 313 (the three loaders) + 417 (card-for-card parity); **error** → 355 (per read, settled) + 354(b) (the undecidable panel) + 325 (no `error.tsx`, the admin root's is neutral); **schema not ready** → 421; **stale** → 353 (the planner beat with `BOOT_GRACE_MS`) + 414 (which tone) + 354(c) (unreadable ≠ healthy); **empty** → 416 with 310's precedence (switch off beats "no bots yet" beats filtered-to-nothing, and a failed read beats all three); **read-only** → 358 (a REMOVED bot: no action buttons, a `neutral` Callout) and, BY CONSTRUCTION, nothing else — `AdminSectionGate` computes `mayAct = ownerOnly ? isAdmin(role) : await canAct(role, domain)` (`admin-section-gate.tsx:46` ✔) and `readOnly = !mayAct && …` (`:47` ✔), and on an Owner-only route the only viewer who gets past the gate is an ADMIN, so `ActReadOnlyBanner` (`:50` ✔) can never render on this console and no page may draw a read-only state of its own; **pending** → 415 (dialog `pending`, scrim and Esc locked while pending) + 412 (`PendingChangesBar`, one guarded form per tab) + 316 (the poller registers nothing while a form is dirty or a dialog is open, read from REACT state) + 414 (a change arriving while dirty shows a Callout instead of refreshing under the operator). ⛔ No surface invents an eighth state, and no state is signalled by colour alone (§A4).

**Why.** The review found "read-only" and "schema not ready" undecided while every other state had a home, and a state with two homes is how `/admin/house` shipped green and wrong twice. Writing the roll-call is also what makes 419's capture list checkable: every state named here has a cell there.

**Files.** `src/components/admin/admin-section-gate.tsx:46`, `:47`, `:49-50` ✔ · `src/components/admin/admin-shell.tsx:408` ✔, `:567` ✔ · `src/components/admin/admin-table-empty.tsx:14` ✔ · `src/components/ui/refresh-poller.tsx:44` ✔.

**Proof.** `test:house-bot-console` 1.422: a roll-call case asserting that for each of the seven states at least one 1.\* case above exercises it (the case names them, so a state losing its test is reported), plus the construction pin — no file under the section renders `ActReadOnlyBanner`, reads `mayAct` or declares a read-only prop (342 already forbids the prop). Plus 419's capture cells, opened and READ.

#### 431. The anchors this review re-derived, and the numbers to use

**Decision.** The anchors below were re-derived by machine on 2026-09-18 against commit `09850526` and the numbers here OUTRANK the same anchor's number anywhere else in this file; the quoted TEXT is what to grep if a line moves again. Every other text-carrying anchor in this file matched at the line it cites ✔. ⛔ A file C5-5b is still editing can move again: the implementer re-greps the TEXT, never the number, and corrects this table in the same commit.

## §3 Build order

Each step ends with `npx tsc --noEmit` at 0, the suites §4 assigns it, and a render opened and READ at 1280 and 360. A step is not finished because it compiles.

**Step 1 — THE PAGE APPEARS IN ADMIN AND RENDERS REAL DATA (today).** Create `src/app/admin/desk/` with three files: `layout.tsx` (the six-line `AdminSectionGate` wrapper, passing the neutral `title` the same commit adds as an optional prop — 301), `page.tsx` (`export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"`, a static neutral `metadata.title`, and as its FIRST statement the session read plus `houseConsoleAudience(session?.userId, "/admin/desk")`, returning `null` when false — 300, 302, 324, 380), and `loading.tsx` (the real neutral `AdminPageHead title="Desk"` plus the strip, band, rail and table ghosts — 313, 417). Make the route reachable and owner-only in the four places that decide it: `NAV_GROUPS` gains `{ href: "/admin/desk", label: "Desk", key: "desk", domain: "ops", ownerOnly: true }` in the Money group after House, `ROUTE_KEYS` gains `["/admin/desk", "desk"]` with no `CRUMB_LABELS` entry (321), `ROUTE_DOMAINS` gains `["/admin/desk", "ops"]` and `OWNER_ONLY_PREFIXES` gains `"/admin/desk"`, with `HOUSE_CONSOLE_PREFIX` added to the gate module as the second belt (322, 341). Add the FIRST gated reader, `houseRosterForConsole(viewerUserId, route)`, to `src/lib/server/house-console-read.ts` with its `CONSOLE_GATES` arity entry and the module header rewritten to name it (340, 372(d)): inside it, ONE `houseBotControlStore.get()` (whose `HouseSchemaNotReady` is caught as 421's STATE, not as a failure), ONE `houseBotStore.listNonRemoved()`, ONE `houseDayBooks(eatDayKey(Date.now()))` and ONE `houseBookStore.openExposure(null)` — **four reads, and NO wallet read: the roster paints no balance and no floor state, so it reads none (356, corrected)** — all in a settled set, returning a painted view model (346, 347, 348, 355, 356, 421). Render, from that real data: `AdminPageHead` with the single "Designate an account" action and its derived disabled reason (314, 403), the master-switch strip with its chip, state sentence and derived unset-limit count (306), the four cap-usage tiles (303, 304, 404), **the line rail carrying only the tab whose panel exists — `roster` — with 319's closed list starting at one entry and growing at steps 3 and 5 (312, corrected: a rail option with no panel is a dead control)**, and the eight-column roster table with money second and third as usage, its cause-naming empty state and its `AdminLoadError` (310, 361, 373, 407). **In the same commit, the rename's whole literal inventory:** all 28 `/admin/house-bots` literals in the seven `src/` files and the shots fixture re-pointed through the one route module, and `COMMIT_7` swapped to `["/admin/desk"]` in the same breath so `test:house-bot-comms` 7.2 and 7.3 both stay green (319, 320, corrected). Add `scripts/house-bot-console.test.mts`, `scripts/red-house-bot-console.mjs` and `scripts/qa-house-bots-visual.mjs` with their three `package.json` keys in this same commit, because this step's own rulings cite them (318). Add the two `REACHED_WITHOUT_NAV` rows and the §4/§5 nav pins (326, 327), and `/admin/desk` to `ADMIN_ROUTES` (329) and to the probe's `MUST_CARRY` (315). Then sign in as Ali, open `/admin/desk`, and READ it at 1280 and 360: the section is in the sidebar, the page paints, and every figure on it came from the database.

**Step 2 — the page is proved gated.** ⛔ The draft's first task here is DONE and is struck: the probe no longer touches the deleted `exposure.ts` — C5-5b re-anchored its ADMIN control to planted house AUDIT rows (357, measured). What remains: add the non-owner staff viewer and the audience-aware classification (393), the `[id]` `FILL` values and the console-unfilled failure (392), the canary amounts (374), and run it; run `test:rbac`, `test:admin-nav`, `test:house-bot-disclosure`, `verify:house-bot-bundle` on a fresh build with its presence control (396), and `qa:admin-view-matrix` (328) or record it NOT MEASURED. Nothing in step 3 starts until 4.1 is 0 with a non-zero ADMIN control on every console instance. ⛔ **Added 2026-09-18 by ruling 506 — SEVEN of the twelve homeless assertions land here, because their subjects are already built and step 2 is the next step that touches them:** **1.384**, **1.386** and **1.389** (the D19 pins 506 names by number — no console client module reaches a house module by value import, type import or copy; the live-refresh trigger names no notification kind; the countdown adds no client module, no house word and no server-config reach), plus **1.332** (X13: a holder who is also an ADMIN stays inside the audience, asserted rather than engineered around — which is exactly what this step's audience-aware classification exercises), **1.342** (the audience verdict is resolved ONCE per render pass, inside the module) **1.343** (the route a gate call names is a STRING LITERAL equal to the file's own console route) and **1.381** (ONE neutral codename, and it is `desk`; the three server-rendered strings are NOT free — this step runs `verify:house-bot-bundle` on a fresh build and the disclosure suite, which is where that assertion can actually fail). ⛔ **And ruling 398's case gains a ROLL-CALL** comparing the D19 section's ACTUAL case ids against 398's own enumerated closed list: today 398 only checks that assertions which EXIST have planted controls, so an assertion that was never written is invisible to the guard that exists to find exactly that — which is how these twelve went missing in the first place.

**Step 3 — the limits tab.** `houseUsageForConsole` (372) with its `CONSOLE_GATES` entry; `ProgressBar`'s `caption` prop with its design-system record (362, 409); the global usage card, the three unset-limit captions and the at/over clause (364, 367); the derived unset count feeding both the strip sentence and the tab badge from ONE read (306, 312); the limits form in `FormColumn measure="form"` at `size="md"` with one guarded form (412). ⛔ **Added 2026-09-18.** **Ruling 473 — build ruling 316's `RefreshPoller` here, with assertion `1.316`:** it is built nowhere and was scheduled in no step (`grep -rn RefreshPoller` returns nothing under `src/app/admin/desk`, and `1.316` appears in no step's "Assertions ADDED" row), so it would have been lost between the steps rather than decided. It belongs to the master-switch strip, and this is the next step that touches the strip — its derived unset-limit count already feeds both the strip sentence and the tab badge from one read. Until it lands the desk is a `force-dynamic` page that refreshes on navigation, which is honest and is what every tile captured so far shows; that gap is TRACKED, not silent. **Ruling 506 — three homeless assertions land here, at the step that first makes each able to fail:** **1.401** (every client file of this section is house-free and the money guards are told how to see it — this step adds the section's FIRST client form, so it is the first step at which the assertion has a population), **1.405** (the tab rail is the kit line variant, URL-backed, 44 px, English sentence case, with counts through `CountBadge` — this step adds the rail's second key and its badge) and **1.406** (the switch, the engine state and every cap breach live ABOVE the rail on every tab — provable only once a second tab exists, which is here). Each is re-run at every later step, not just this one.

**Step 4 — the bot page.** `houseDetailForConsole` (358), the strip with status chip, reason and way out from the one server map (311, 413), the balance-FLOOR STATE sentence (368), the "Limit usage" card's five money rows and two count rows (363, 351), the engine-health Callout from the durable planner beat with its boot grace and its unreadable-state branch (309, 352, 353, 354, 414), the action row's dialogs with explicit tones and neutral typed words (388, 415), and the read-only REMOVED state (358). ⛔ **Added 2026-09-18 — five scheduling rulings land on this step, and each names what it builds:**
- **Ruling 507 — X6 and X1's duty-name half are BUILT here; X2, X4 and X5 are STRUCK (§6).** X6 is the settlement-blocked chip: a missing holder wallet blocks settlement for **every player in that market**, and the register itself says that must not live only in an alert — a condition that stops other people's money is a console STATE, not a notification. X1's duty-name half rides the same heartbeat-row `extra` field and is built with it.
- **Ruling 508 — the detail route's `rules` and `targets` panels and the "Enter now" control are SCHEDULED, not struck:** rules and targets with this page, "Enter now" with this action row. §5 already captures all three and step 7 must open and READ that capture set, so leaving them unbuilt would have made step 7's own gate unmeetable; their content is specified at lower rank (`03-design-spec.md:249`, `:260-271`, `:276`), they are already inside PROGRESS's Commit 7 scope, and bells and letters that already shipped link to `?tab=rules` — striking them would break live hrefs to fix a drafting gap. Ruling 319's closed tab list grows to hold them at this step, never ahead of them.
- **Ruling 514 — A24's poller-failure alert is WIRED here.** Measured: `POLLER_FAILURE_ALERT_AFTER = 10` has exactly one occurrence in the whole tree (its own definition) and `ALERT_KEY.pollerFailing` has no writer, so the alert is dead end to end inside a commit marked ✅ CLOSED. It is not struck, because a silently failing poller is precisely the condition the engine cannot otherwise report, and this step's engine-health Callout (309, 352, 353, 354, 414) is its natural consumer and reader.
- **Ruling 504 — `lastStoppedAt` and `veto` are decided HERE or deleted here.** This step's refusal copy is their only candidate consumer: it either calls each by name with a behavioural case, or deletes it from the interface and both twins in this same commit, with both names added to `dal-parity.test.mts`'s `NEVER` array. ⛔ Not carried past this step undecided a second time.
- **Ruling 506 — two homeless assertions land here:** **1.350** (the named list of DAL members Commit 7 needs, and the exhaustive list it does not — the member-by-member decision above is exactly this step's) and **1.408** (money is `.amount` with the platform helpers, and no surface renders a total, net or aggregate — this step builds the five money rows that give the assertion its sharpest population).

## §5 The visual capture list

**Widths (all mandatory): 360, 640, 768, 1024, 1280, 1920.** 320 as an overflow control only. Locale: English, plus SW and ZH at 360 for the activity tab's filter rail alone (419).

**Surfaces × states.**
1. `/admin/desk?tab=roster` × switch OFF · switch ON · auto-off GLOBAL_LOSS_STOP · auto-off ENGINE_FAULT · auto-off SUNSET · engine stale · a money cap unset · **two of `REQUIRED_FOR_MASTER_ON` unset (the "Set 2 global limits first →" strip, 306)** · a failed roster read · **the house schema not present (421's neutral Callout, no band, no Toggle)** · 0 bots · 1 bot · 5 bots covering ACTIVE/PAUSED/AUTO_PAUSED · roster full (the disabled head action).
2. `/admin/desk?tab=activity` × rows · 0 rows · filtered to 0 · a failed count read · page 2 · every filter set at once.
3. `/admin/desk?tab=limits` × all limits set · one unset (each of the three caption branches) · usage at the limit · usage over the limit · a failed usage read.
4. `/admin/desk?tab=history` × rows · 0 rows · page 2.
5. `/admin/desk/new` × find · check with blocking rows · check with warnings only · consent · review · roster full.
6. `/admin/desk/[id]?tab=overview|rules|targets|activity|history` × PAUSED(NEW) · ACTIVE · AUTO_PAUSED(PASSWORD_CHANGED) · REMOVED (read-only).
7. Dialogs, in the real-route client pass at 360/768/1280 only (the static harness prints NOT MEASURED): Master ON · kill-switch OFF · Start · Pause · Re-verify · Remove · Cancel intent · Enter now (Placing and Retrying).
8. The restricted panel, as a non-ADMIN staff viewer, on all three paths including one with a real `hb_` id (301).
9. Every `loading.tsx`, captured mid-load, against its page for a card-for-card jump (417).

**What is being LOOKED FOR, not just captured:** a clipped or wrapped TZS figure; a money cell scrolled out of view at 360; two amounts in one tile; a caption that does not name its cap; an empty state that does not name its cause; a failed read painted as a zero; a load→loaded jump; a 44 px control rendered at 32 or 40; a Swahili or Chinese filter label overflowing its dense pill; a house word anywhere on a screen a non-owner can reach.

## §6 Dropped — mechanisms and clauses struck, and why

No ruling NUMBER was dropped: both "REFUTED" verdicts (301, 317) and the two REFUTED-in-substance ones (364, 372) supplied replacement text for the same subject, and a refuted mechanism is not an abandoned decision (§0). What follows is every mechanism actually struck.

1. **`/admin/house-bots` as the route segment** (area 1's paths; area 5's `exposure` codename) — dropped for `/admin/desk`: the RBAC and nav tables that must hold the prefix are both measured client-reachable and both ship verbatim in a public chunk, and `exposure` collides with the "Open exposure" figure on every tab and with the struck S6 surface.
2. **`[botId]` as the dynamic segment** — dropped for `[id]`: the comms href resolver allows exactly `["[id]", "[positionId]", "[marketId]", "[slug]"]` ✔, so `[botId]` would report the console's own notifier hrefs as unresolvable.
3. **300's `houseConsoleOwner` bespoke export, 340's THUNK door, and 343's header-derived route** — dropped: the gate-call pin fixes the arity, requires the signed-in session's id as arg 0 and a STRING LITERAL route as arg 1 ✔, so none of the three could be called at all. Replaced by query-shaped named readers (340, 372).
4. **372's page-built read** — dropped for the same reason plus `CONSOLE_HOUSE_READ_ALLOWED`'s single entry ✔: a console page cannot name `book.ts` to build a thunk.
5. **300's claim that passing the route literal "writes the string into `roles.ts`"** — dropped as false; the real reasons are the grant-table path and the client-reachability of `roles.ts`.
6. **301's premise that a missing `layout.tsx` turns `test:admin-section-gate` red** — dropped: a page that renders the gate itself satisfies §0. The layout is kept for what it PAINTS, not for a suite.
7. **306's "PLAN §15 X5's premise is FALSE" deviation** — dropped: X5 is about call sites and there is none; the only `tone="gold"` in `src/` is a `<Stat>`.
8. **306's `test:gold-is-money` proof line** — dropped: that suite is scoped to two identity files.
9. **317's `AdminPagination` over a keyset page** — dropped: the pager requires `total` ✔ and draws numbered links ✔, while `Page<T>` carries no total ✔.
10. **Two paging idioms on one page** (numbered for the feed, cursor links for the history) — dropped for ONE idiom: numbered, with counting + offset readers in both twins.
11. **303's denominator amount in a tile's `delta`** — dropped: the delta span is a letter-spaced `text-micro` rung ✔ and §M4 forbids tracking over an amount.
12. **362(b)'s blanket "no money in any `AdminKpi`"** — narrowed: one compact amount in the `value` slot is legal; no amount in the `delta`, and never two in a tile.
13. **404's "Bets today" tile** — dropped from the band for 303's "Stake today": bets-per-day usage lives on the limits tab, and the band is the three money caps that refuse plus the roster count.
14. **364's blanket per-bot "this bot cannot place a bet" caption** — dropped for the `CLEAR_EXEMPT` fields, which stop targeted and manual stakes only; the blanket form would be a lie.
15. **310's "Live balance is kept on S3's strip" compensation** — dropped: 368 strikes the balance there too, so the compensating controls are the feed's `CAP_BALANCE_FLOOR` sentence and the floor STATE sentence.
16. **The S3 `money` tab** (its rows, its columns, its empty state, `moneyTab`, `txnPageForUser`, `positionsForUser`) — dropped, with `03:272`, `03:274` and `PLAN.md:443` struck in place.
17. **`houseBotBook`, `HouseBotBook`, `netTzs`, `feeWithheldTzs` and ruling 183's fee derivation** — dropped: ZERO callers measured anywhere.
18. **310's promise of a REMOVED bot reachable from the roster** — dropped: `listNonRemoved` excludes it ✔; its doors are the history row and a typed id (358).

23. **392(d)'s "the count must exceed the PROGRESS-recorded one"** — dropped: a gate not in the pipeline is not a gate; the floor rises in the script.
24. **394's `workers`-based action selection** — dropped: measured many-to-many, with 110 workers on shared player-callable actions; selection is by the manifest's `filename`.
25. **395's assertion over `API_PATHS`** — dropped: that fixture already contains the string `house-liquidity`; the assertion is over the walked inventory.

27. **413's royal PENDING** — dropped for PLAN §8's neutral, which creates no collision.

29. **403's `Matukio` gloss** — dropped: measured 0 occurrences in both shipped sources.
30. **419's blanket English-only capture set** — narrowed: the dense filter rail translates and is captured in SW and ZH at 360.

Every one is replaced by a named assertion that can.

**Struck by the completeness review (2026-09-18), each measured against the working tree:**

Replaced by the ONE named change to `16.d20`'s `NEVER` array plus three new cases.

That is the dead-control class ruling 432(a) already refuses: a surface that reports a condition but offers no way to change it teaches the reader to stop looking at it, and every neighbouring control loses force with it. (X6 and X1's duty-name half survive and are BUILT at step 4: a missing holder wallet blocks settlement for every player in that market, which is a console STATE, not a notification.)
46. **X4, the bounds-change callout** — struck, same reason as X2: no control on the console acts on it.
